integer lda, complex*16, dimension( ldb, * ) b, integer ldb) DESCRIPTION The routines in this library perform matrix multiply and vector operations for complex*16 precision floating-point numbers. They are designed for testing the BLAS routines. zchk1-zchk6 These routines are designed to test the three-argument routines of the BLAS. zmake This routine generates a matrix with a specific type. zmmch This routine checks the matrix multiplication of two matrices. zmmtch This routine checks the matrix multiplication of two matrices with a specific upper or lower triangular structure. zsetmat This routine sets up matrices for testing BLAS routines. zsetran This routine sets up matrices with random values for testing BLAS routines. ztrans This routine transposes a matrix. zxmake This routine generates a matrix with a specific type. zxmat This routine checks the matrix multiplication of two matrices. zxtrans This routine transposes a matrix. DIAGNOSTICS The routines return 0 if no errors were found, and a positive integer indicating the number of errors found. AUTHOR This man page was written by Julien Puydt .
